Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the rooms space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 638 and the convergence rate is O(85/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the rooms space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 638 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 85. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any rooms module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 755. The projective resolution has length at most 149 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on rooms structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 755. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 149. QED.
